# IPM case 4: isolated 860-generator expansion and research campaign

Status: ENDPOINT_NOT_REACHED. Neither an exact PDE profile nor a stationary optimizer is established; every state found is a numerical approximation, and in each of the first eighty-eight accepted states (all those checked) the unnormalized density residual still grows at infinity. The stages the prompt fixes before that endpoint (isolated copy, Linux qualification and accepted278 reproduction, exact 860 embedding, 860 seed, guarded optimization) were carried out and checked independently, with the qualification failures and exceptions detailed below. The long optimization was terminated at the end of the run and is not left running.

Run record: T0 = 2026-10-24T03:05Z (working copy created, start of the isolated campaign); T_end = 2026-10-26T13:43:03Z (date -u at the close of this report); elapsed time from T0 to T_end 58.63 h. The working sessions and all campaign processes, the long optimization included, were stopped between 2026-10-26T13:28:17Z and 13:29:02Z at the run's time cap. Results are fixed at the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z unless dated otherwise. This is the first published report for this case.

Paths (realpaths):
- SOURCE_REALPATH = /mnt/build/pde-exploring-20261015/repo/Changhe/IPM/case4/IPM_StageXIII_GitHub_handoff (read only)
- WORK_REALPATH = /mnt/build/pde-exploring-20261015/c4/c4-num/work
- OUTPUT_REALPATH = /mnt/build/pde-exploring-20261015/c4/c4-num/outputs

## Summary for the reader

- Verdict: NO on item 10 of the prompt. A theorem excludes exact profiles anywhere in the 860 family, so optimizing in this family cannot produce one; the optimizer states are not shown to be stationary, and exact computations show several of them, taken as exact finite potentials, are not.
- Proved results and their exact scope (each checked independently; the theorem also by an independent proof review):
  - Embedding: all 186 old generators lie in the span of the 860 new ones, through a coefficient map of rank 186 that does not depend on c_l, checked in exact rational arithmetic. The center relation and the five Taylor conditions have rank 6 for every c_l > 1 and a_0 != 0, so there are (860 + 1) - 6 = 855 feasible variables, with c_l and a_0 free and no gauge direction removed.
  - No exact profile in the family: no potential with finitely many angular modes of the 860 form, radial functions of class C^4 away from the origin, bounded near the origin and growing subquartically, is an exact profile with nonzero density. Every 860 generator grows subquartically when c_l > 1, so no member of the 860 family with c_l > 1 and a_0 != 0 is exact.
  - Not stationary: exact derivatives show that states 6, 8, 10, 16, 60 and the handoff state 88, each taken as an exact finite potential, are not stationary on the set that holds its a_0, center relation and five saved Taylor defects fixed, and that exactly Taylor-feasible rational references of states 18 and 88 are not stationary on the exact center-and-five-Taylor set. Stationarity of the floating-point optimizer states themselves is not decided.
  - Certified suprema: for the exact potentials reconstructed from states 30, 48, 82 and 88, with the center coefficient eliminated exactly, sup E_f = sup E_rho is enclosed in an interval of width 1e-18 and attained on the vertical axis; for state 88 it lies strictly between 1.2196685035505e-5 and 1.2196685035506e-5. For states 18 and 22 the points attaining the suprema are located on the vertical axis and the suprema are bounded above (section 7).
- Best numerical evidence (data cut, checked independently): accepted state 88 of the 860 run (trial 103; 88 of 104 trials accepted) has J_abs = 2.7441060958610226e-11, a certified decrease of more than 99.35 percent from the seed's 4.2683726070956797e-9 (exact rational enclosures of width 1e-145; printed decimals are rounded), c_l = 1.8085383949271123, a_0 = 10.29757491765872, the five Taylor defects below 3e-12, sup E_f = sup E_rho about 1.21967e-5 (the baseline's sampled E_f is 1.23849639744e-4), and far-field norms ||E_1|| = 0.121126, ||E_2|| = 2.58698, ||E_3|| = 18.4807.
- After the data cut (taken from the run record only, not checked independently): the run continued to 156 trials with 131 accepted states; the last, state 131, has J_abs = 1.2673066866466526e-11 by the run's whole-axis evaluation, c_l = 1.810319210199998 and a_0 = 10.300294891831177.
- Baseline and qualification: on this host accepted278 gives J_abs = 4.268372607095559e-9 (stored 4.2683726070948805e-9) and sampled maxima that agree with the documented ones in their first eleven significant digits. The strict bitwise replay of the stored Mac residual fails (difference norm 2.17e-15 against the unchanged threshold 1.31e-18) and is kept as a failure; a new reference epoch was adopted for the same physical state. The 860 seed reproduces the old training residual to 3.3e-11 relative and gives J_abs = 4.268372607095638e-9 against 4.268372607095585e-9 for the old representation. The feasible-chart gate passed 27 of 29 checks; the two failures are finite-difference checks along three numerically null directions, run under a recorded exception.
- Source protection: every comparison, the last by the independent verifier at 2026-10-26T13:21:45Z and by the coordinating session at 13:23:08Z, found all 219 files of the protected tree unchanged. No recorded operation wrote into it; one git status query at 2026-10-24T23:40:39Z rewrote the index file of the enclosing source repository, outside the protected tree (section 2).
- Hours: elapsed wall-clock time from T0 to T_end is 58.63 h. Verified recorded compute (the union of the intervals of all recorded runs, reproduced independently) was 45.04 h at the data cut and 49.08 h at the time-only cut 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z (68.10 h with parallel runs summed); optimizer worker wall time was 24.28 h and optimizer CPU time 97.48 h in completed slices at that cut. Time after 08:00:00Z is not counted. These are recorded intervals, not measures of useful work; active investigator time is not measured.
- 48-hour campaign: the 48-hour elapsed mark passed at 2026-10-26T03:05Z and the recorded computation union passed 48 h by 08:00:00Z, while the same supervised optimization process ran from 2026-10-25T07:24:21Z to the stop. That these were 48 hours of genuine, useful work is a judgment from the dated records of the three working sessions (computation, independent verification, coordination), not a measurement.
- Long run: the detached, supervised run (5,000 s slices, 8 workers, 250 h guarded budget) was checked independently to be running at 2026-10-26T13:20:00Z. The end-of-run shutdown terminated it at about 13:28Z (SIGTERM, then SIGKILL, to all campaign processes), and none of its three identified processes (supervisor, guard and campaign worker) existed when the process table was read at 13:30:12Z (an observation of the coordinating session, not checked independently). Its committed states survive in the campaign directory under OUTPUT_REALPATH; a restart is possible in principle but was not tested after this kind of termination (section 9). At the data cut the rest of the budget corresponded to about 981 to 1,193 further trials and at least 9.3 days of gapless guarded running (arithmetic on measured rates, not a forecast). The time to convergence is unknown and cannot be guaranteed.
- Exact remaining gap: (1) an exact profile needs a potential outside the theorem's class (infinitely many angular modes, or growth or regularity outside its hypotheses); inside the 860 family the residual can be made small but not zero. (2) The far field is unresolved: E_1 != 0 in each of the first eighty-eight accepted states (all those checked), so R_rho grows like r^(2/c_l - 1) (exponent 0.10587 at state 88), and part of E_1 lies above angular harmonic 19 (3.329 percent of ||E_1|| at state 88), a part that comes from the branch-0 part of E_1 alone and that the branch-1 moments of the m <= 9 family cannot produce, and so cannot cancel. (3) No state is shown to be stationary, no basis convergence is shown, and the continuum suprema are enclosed to width 1e-18 for four states only (and located, with upper bounds, for states 18 and 22 as well).
- Best next step: a separately named exploratory branch that extends branch 1 alone to angular modes m <= 19, imposes E_1 = 0 exactly (twenty conditions, linear in the branch-1 moments at fixed c_l and branch 0), minimizes the same J_abs and reports the maxima of the unnormalized density residual. It changes the problem and is not comparable with the fixed-objective campaign (section 10).
- The accompanying artifacts hold the run's documents, the new code, manifests, the 860 seed and the state-88 checkpoint, with exact commands in their README.

## Answers to the ten questions of the prompt

Dates. Results are at the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z, except the result of the standalone assessment of the handoff state, which was launched after the cut and is given with its own read times (item 9 and section 9), the time figures of a second, time-only cut at 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z (item 6 and section 6), and the record of the end of the run (sections 2, 6 and 9). Each result was checked independently unless the text says that it is taken from the run record only. The exact computations on the handoff state 88 in items 7 and 10 and sections 7 and 10 were made after the data cut, on the state fixed at the cut, and are given with their dates.

Short answers. Sections 1 to 10 give the evidence and the exact scope of every number; where a short answer and a section differ in detail, the section is authoritative.

1. Locations. The original handoff is at SOURCE_REALPATH and was only read. The independent working copy is WORK_REALPATH and the campaign outputs are under OUTPUT_REALPATH. Neither lies inside, or links into, the protected case 4 tree (section 1).

2. Source protection. Checked independently, before and during the campaign: every comparison found all 219 regular files of the protected case 4 tree unchanged (SHA-256, size, mode, device, inode, link count), and the tree has no symbolic links. No recorded operation wrote into the protected tree. One operation wrote outside it, into the git metadata of the enclosing source repository: at 2026-10-24T23:40:39Z a git status query aimed at the handoff directory rewrote that repository's index file (section 2). The comparisons after the 48-hour elapsed mark, by the coordinating session at 2026-10-26T03:05:11Z and by the verification session at 03:05:18Z, also found all 219 files unchanged and no symbolic links. The last comparisons, at the end of the run, by the verification session at 2026-10-26T13:21:45Z and by the coordinating session at 13:23:08Z, found the same. Hash comparisons cannot exclude a write that was later reverted.

3. Linux qualification and accepted278. Passed, with one strict failure kept as a failure. On this host accepted278 gives J_abs = 4.268372607095559e-9 (stored 4.2683726070948805e-9, difference 6.8e-22), sampled E_f = 1.23849639743e-4 and E_rho = 7.16582921426e-5 (documented 1.23849639744e-4 and 7.16582921424e-5), the stored c_l and a_0 = 14.76772999060648 recomputed from the coefficients (documented 14.767729990606481). The bitwise-level replay of the stored Mac residual vector fails: difference norm 2.17e-15 against the unchanged threshold 1.31e-18. The old run therefore cannot be resumed exactly on this host, and a new reference epoch was adopted for the same physical state (section 3).

4. Embedding and feasible dimension. Yes. All 186 old generators lie exactly in the span of the 860 new ones, through a coefficient map of rank 186 that does not depend on c_l, checked in exact rational arithmetic. The center relation and the five Taylor conditions have rank 6 for every c_l > 1 and a_0 != 0, so the true number of feasible variables is (860 coefficients + c_l) - 6 = 855. a_0 stays free and no gauge direction is removed (section 4).

5. Reproduction by the 860 seed. Yes, within the qualified tolerances. The seed's training-mesh residual differs from the old evaluator's by 3.3e-11 relative (limit 1e-9); its whole-axis J_abs is 4.268372607095638e-9 against 4.268372607095585e-9 for the old representation; the sampled maxima agree within 3.1e-12 relative; the seed reloads and reproduces its residual bitwise. The later full gate of the feasible chart failed 2 of its 29 checks, both finite-difference checks along three numerically null directions, and the campaign proceeds under a recorded exception for exactly those directions (sections 4 and 5).

6. Verified research and compute hours. From T0 to T_end the elapsed wall-clock time is 58.63 h. No figure below extends past the time-only cut 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z. The 48-hour elapsed and recorded-computation thresholds were met; 48 hours of genuine, useful work remain a qualitative judgment, not a verified duration (section 6). At the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z, reproduced independently: 48.92 h of elapsed wall-clock time; 45.04 h with at least one recorded computation running (62.75 h if parallel runs are summed); 20.20 h of optimizer worker wall time and 82.80 h of optimizer CPU time in completed slices. These are recorded intervals, not continuous useful work or active investigator time. A separate statistic, reproduced independently, uses only the timestamps of the transcripts of all working, helper and consultation sessions, not their content. After sorting the timestamps within each transcript and taking the union of intervals between adjacent records at most 610 seconds apart, recorded session timestamp coverage leaves 1.951 seconds uncovered in the 48 hours 55 minutes elapsed at the 04:00:00Z cut; the coverage is 48.12 hours with a 120-second threshold. These statistics include waiting and polling and do not measure useful work or active investigator time. The 48-hour elapsed mark, 2026-10-26T03:05Z, passed before the data cut, but the recorded computation union at the cut, 45.04 h, is below 48 h. At the second, time-only cut 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z, which adds no state, trial or result and only measures time, the same quantities, also reproduced independently, were 52.92 h of elapsed wall-clock time, 49.08 h with at least one recorded computation running (68.10 h if parallel runs are summed), 24.28 h of optimizer worker wall time and 97.48 h of optimizer CPU time in completed slices. So the recorded computation union reached 48 h by 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z. It is a recorded interval union, not a measure of useful work: of the 45.04 h at the 04:00:00Z cut, 39.98 h were covered by successful guarded runs or by unguarded calculations of the ledger and 5.06 h only by guarded runs that did not succeed, and neither part measures useful or useless work; active investigator time between records is not measured (section 6).

7. 860 optimization. A trust-region optimization of the unchanged objective J_abs over the 855 feasible variables, started from the exactly embedded seed: a first pilot, closed after one accepted trial for a defect in its worker-pool shutdown; a repaired pilot of thirteen trials that met its pass criteria; and from 2026-10-25T07:24:21Z the long run, which continues the same run (sections 7 and 9). At the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z, checked independently through trial 104 of the repaired run, the last trial committed before the cut:
   - 88 of 104 trials were accepted. J_abs fell from 4.2683726070956797e-9 at the seed to 2.7441060958610226e-11 at state 88, a certified decrease of more than 99.35 percent. Each of these J_abs values has an exact rational enclosure of width 1e-145; the printed decimals are rounded values, not enclosures. At state 88, c_l = 1.8085383949271123 and a_0 = 10.29757491765872. The sixteen rejected trials, trials 9, 61, 65, 68, 71, 74, 77, 80, 83, 86, 89, 92, 95, 98, 101 and 104, raised J_abs over their incumbents by about 0.98, 5.39, 2.87, 1.91, 1.28, 0.89, 0.67, 0.59, 0.61, 0.70, 0.83, 1.00, 1.18, 1.36, 1.51 and 1.64 percent, and each quartered the trust radius.
   - In these accepted states the largest of the five Taylor defects is below 3e-12 and the recorded KKT residual of the trust-region subproblem at most 3.6e-11.
   - Residual maxima: certified point values, which are lower bounds for the continuum suprema, exist for accepted states 1, 6, 16, 18, 22, 30, 44, 48, 82 and 88, and exact whole-plane upper bounds for states 6, 8, 10, 13, 15, 16, 18, 22, 30, 44, 48, 59, 62, 65, 73, 82 and 88. For state 30, sup E_f = sup E_rho, strictly between 2.7704210803831e-5 and 2.7704210803832e-5, attained on the vertical axis. For state 48, sup E_f = sup E_rho, strictly between 1.9342743879628e-5 and 1.9342743879629e-5, attained at exactly two points of the vertical axis. For state 82, sup E_f = sup E_rho, strictly between 1.2811732253717e-5 and 1.2811732253718e-5, attained at exactly two points of the vertical axis. For the handoff state 88, sup E_f = sup E_rho, strictly between 1.2196685035505e-5 and 1.2196685035506e-5, attained at exactly two points of the vertical axis; both suprema of state 88 are smaller than those of state 82 by more than 4.80 percent, and the maximizing radius of state 88 is larger than that of state 82 by more than 0.89 percent (exact computations made on 2026-10-26 between 05:35Z and 05:59Z, after the 04:00Z data cut, on state 88, the state fixed at the cut by the cut rule, and on the saved state 82, checked independently; the point check was at a location chosen by the standalone assessment). Both suprema of states 6, 8, 10, 13, 15, 16 and 18 are certified smaller than those of the seed, both suprema of state 30 smaller than those of states 18 and 22, both suprema of state 44 smaller than those of state 30, both suprema of state 48 smaller than those of state 44, both suprema of states 59, 62, 65 and 73 smaller than those of state 48, and both suprema of state 82 smaller than those of state 48 by more than 33.76 percent. Sampled maxima are lower estimates, not certified values.
   - Far field at state 88: ||E_1|| = 0.121126, ||E_2|| = 2.58698, ||E_3|| = 18.4807 (item 8).
   - At state 30 the run's unweighted rounding indicator, a sampled diagnostic, is exceeded at five of the 90,624 training nodes; the weighted inequalities used in the objective's error estimate hold there, and the run continued unchanged (section 7).
   These states are approximations whose density residual still grows at infinity; they are not exact PDE solutions, and their stationarity has not been established.

8. Growth of the unnormalized density residual. Yes, it still grows. For the baseline, R_rho grows like r^0.1056 (exponent 2/c_l - 1) with leading angular coefficient E_1 of L2 norm 0.4404, of which 0.1446% lies in odd harmonics 21 to 39; these come from the branch-0 part of E_1 alone and cannot be produced by the branch-1 moments (section 10). In each of the first eighty-eight accepted states E_1 is nonzero and the exponent 2/c_l - 1 stays between 0.10558 and 0.10665; c_l fell at every accepted step from state 5 to state 50 and rose at every accepted step from state 51 to state 88, so the exponent rose to 0.10664 at state 50 and then fell, to 0.10587 at state 88. ||E_1|| falls from 0.44023 at state 1 to 0.12113 at state 88, while the norm of its part above harmonic 19, as a fraction of ||E_1||, grows from 0.166 percent at state 1 to 3.510 percent at state 61 and then falls at every later state, to 3.329 percent at state 88; ||E_2|| and ||E_3|| are largest at state 6, slightly above their seed values, and fall at every later accepted state, to 2.5870 and 18.4807 at state 88. These trends do not show which harmonics account for the decrease of J_abs, and far-field norms are not a proxy for J_abs (section 8).

9. Long-running process. No long run is left active at T_end. The long run was a detached supervised process (supervisor 1270876, started 2026-10-25T07:24:21Z with setsid and nohup at nice 19, 5,000 s slices, 8 workers, no trial cap, read-only code tree) in its own directory under OUTPUT_REALPATH. It was checked independently to be running at 2026-10-26T13:20:00Z and was terminated at about 13:28Z by the end-of-run shutdown of all campaign processes (section 9). The rest of this item describes the run at the data cut. At the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z the handoff state was accepted state 88 from trial 103 (commit event 154, committed 672.6 s before the cut), the accepted commit event with the greatest sequence number whose recorded commit time and event file time were both before the cut; its snapshot and arrays files have SHA-256 70b06bc6c95869a2ef0bf5dcc6e8bdfc605fab4399a89752dc89b8346abfa4ab and 81e7825571f72706982042d4b178b75a6101f4c1caeac31bbd4a66be0d681035, both recomputed independently. The supervisor log, the committed events, the accepted snapshots, the guard session and the assessment ledger are in the run directory (section 9). At the cut the guard ledger held 43 completed guarded runs totalling 93,353.086 s (25.931 h), so at most 223.56 h of the 250 h guarded budget remained after the 0.517 h that the slice then in progress had run; read at 04:00:05Z, 89.6 GiB of the 875.0 GiB shared file system were free. ETA at the data cut, as arithmetic on measured rates and not as a forecast: over the complete slices 7 to 21 the run made 5.335 trials per hour of slice guard time and 4.388 per hour of all guarded time of the long run, so the rest of the budget corresponds to about 981 to 1,193 further trials, and it cannot be used up before 2026-11-04T11:33:05Z, about 9.3 days after the cut, the time reached if guarded runs follow each other without gaps. The retained launch record and guard session document one standalone full assessment of state 88 launched at 2026-10-26T04:00:37Z, under a fresh guard session separate from the optimization run (section 9). It finished at 2026-10-26T04:56:39Z after 3,362 s of guarded wall time, with guard success, return code 0 and all recorded guard decisions no-stop; its record is complete with no failed check and requests no scientific stop. Its tight integral of state 88, 2.7441060958610037e-11 with a stated error of 3.6e-22, lies 1.9e-25 below the exact enclosure of the objective of state 88, and its largest sampled E_f = E_rho, 1.2196685033154225e-5 after local refinement, is a value at a stored sample point, not a bound. These values were checked independently within a finite numerical scope (section 9). Measured up to the data cut: the first six slices of the long run held six trials each, at 5.23 to 5.84 trials per hour of guarded slice time, the seventh, with longer trial walls of 741 to 839 s while the shared host was more heavily loaded, held five, at 4.35 trials per hour, the eighth and ninth held six each, at 5.28 and 5.52 trials per hour of guarded slice time, two of the ninth slice's six trials being rejected, the tenth held five, at 4.75 trials per hour, the eleventh six, at 5.06 trials per hour, the twelfth six, at 5.17 trials per hour, two trials of each of these three being rejected, the thirteenth held five, at 4.87 trials per hour of guarded slice time, one of the five (trial 86) being rejected, the fourteenth held six, at 4.96 trials per hour of guarded slice time, two of the six (trials 89 and 92) being rejected, and the fifteenth held seven, at 6.59 trials per hour of guarded slice time, three of the seven (trials 95, 98 and 101) being rejected, and the six full assessments, each due when J_abs halves, took 2,834 to 3,994 s. With constant trial walls of 600 to 940 s a slice holds six to four trials, so 100 trials take about 17.5 to 27.4 hours plus the full assessments. The configured budget is 250 h of guarded run time. These are times to spend a trial budget. The time to convergence is unknown and cannot be guaranteed.

10. Stationarity and exact solutions. No. Neither a stationary optimizer nor an exact PDE profile is supported by the evidence.
   - The baseline is provably not stationary in the finite family. Exact derivatives show that states 6, 8, 10, 16 and 60 and the handoff state 88, each taken as an exact finite potential, are not stationary on the set that holds its a_0, center relation and five saved Taylor defects fixed, and that an exactly Taylor-feasible rational reference of state 18, and the minimum of J_abs on one exact line through it, are not stationary on the exact center-and-five-Taylor set, and neither is an exactly Taylor-feasible rational reference of the handoff state 88 (computed after the data cut on the state fixed at the cut). The stationarity of the optimizer states themselves has not been established.
   - A theorem, checked by an independent proof review, excludes exact profiles in the family: no potential with finitely many angular modes of the form used by the 860 family, radial functions of class C^4 away from the origin, bounded near the origin and growing subquartically is an exact profile with nonzero density. Every 860 generator grows subquartically when c_l > 1, so no member of the 860 family with c_l > 1 and nonzero central density slope is an exact profile.
   - In an explicit comparison family outside the 860 span, E_1 can be cancelled at an objective cost that vanishes as the radial degree K grows, but the maxima of the unnormalized density residual then grow like K^(1/c_l - 1/2): the residual is moved outward, not removed.
   - Best next step: a separately named exploratory branch that extends branch 1 alone to angular modes m <= 19, imposes E_1 = 0 exactly (twenty conditions, linear in the branch-1 moments at fixed c_l and branch 0), minimizes the same J_abs and reports the maxima of the unnormalized density residual. It changes the problem and is not comparable with the fixed-objective campaign (section 10).

## 1. Original handoff and working copy
The original handoff is the read-only tree at SOURCE_REALPATH above. In every recorded operation of this campaign it was only read and checksummed; no recorded command executed, imported or wrote it. One git status query aimed at it wrote the index file of the enclosing repository, outside the tree but inside the source repository (section 2). All 217 handoff files (no symlinks) were copied to WORK_REALPATH; every file's SHA-256 matches the source and every copied file is a distinct inode. The numerical campaign's outputs are under OUTPUT_REALPATH; selected records are in the accompanying artifacts. An independent second copy was made for verification, with the same 217 hashes. (Verified.)

## 2. Source protection before and after
Before any work, an independent before-manifest of all 219 regular files of the protected case 4 tree (217 of them in the handoff) was recorded: SHA-256, size, mode, device and inode, link count. The tree has no symbolic links. Full interim repeats at 2026-10-24T03:26:17Z, 04:50:29Z and 05:49:16Z found all 219 files unchanged. (Verified.) Further full repeats by the verification session, about hourly up to 2026-10-24T21:53:39Z and again at 23:42:29Z after the incident below, and separate comparisons by the coordinating session at 2026-10-24T21:04:46Z and 22:05:38Z against the same before-manifest (content hashes, sizes, modes, device, inode and link counts) also found all 219 files unchanged and no symbolic links. The comparisons after the 48-hour elapsed mark, by the coordinating session at 2026-10-26T03:05:11Z and by the verification session at 03:05:18Z, against the same before-manifest, also found all 219 files unchanged and no symbolic links. The last comparisons, at the end of the run, by the verification session at 2026-10-26T13:21:45Z and by the coordinating session at 13:23:08Z, against the same before-manifest, also found all 219 files unchanged and no symbolic links. Unchanged hashes show matching file contents at the comparison times. They cannot exclude a transient write that was later reverted. No recorded operation of this campaign wrote into the protected tree or was designed to: all recorded execution used the working copy and the separate verification copy.

Incident (disclosed). At 2026-10-24T23:40:39Z the coordinating session ran a git status query aimed at the protected handoff directory, intending only to read. The handoff has no git metadata of its own, so git used the repository that encloses it and, as an automatic metadata refresh, rewrote that repository's index file. This file lies outside the protected case 4 tree but inside the source repository, where the rules forbid any write, and it is the git metadata that records the protected tree, which must not be updated. A git status query does not modify tracked files. A comparison against the before-manifest immediately afterwards, at 23:40:47Z, found all 219 protected files unchanged in content, size, mode, device, inode and link count, with no symbolic links. The earlier bytes of the index file were not recorded anywhere, so the change to it cannot be described. No git command was run in the source repository after this, and the index file was not restored, because restoring it would be a second write. The verification session examined the index file afterwards without running git: its size (1,807,894 bytes) and modification time (23:40:39Z) match this account and no lock file remains. Its independent full comparison at 23:42:29Z found all 219 protected files unchanged. Without a record of the earlier bytes, the exact change to the index file remains undetermined.

## 3. Linux qualification and accepted278 reconstruction
Linux qualification of the baseline passed, except for the strict replay, which failed and is kept as a failure. Verified results:
- Physical parameters of accepted278 (file SHA-256 812d34466c078e2ce643c49671299f4359f764fe2e1d925426762ba962d6e955, confirmed): c_l = 1.8089923602744993 as stored, and a_0 = 14.76772999060648155 recomputed independently from the coefficients by an ordinary Taylor expansion with analytic center elimination, in agreement with the documented 14.767729990606481.
- Repository verification, relocation inspect, restart audit and smoke test pass in the copy.
- The fresh independent whole-axis objective of accepted278 on this host is J_abs = 4.268372607095559e-9 (integration error diagnostic 2.87e-19) against the stored 4.2683726070948805e-9, a difference of 6.8e-22. An independent 85-digit evaluation with exact angular integration gives 4.26837260709580205558e-9 on log r in [-25, 60]; the omitted ends are bounded rigorously by 2.9e-73 (origin) and 8.9e-36 (infinity).
- The unchanged strict replay (residual vector difference <= 64 eps times the norm) FAILS on this host: the difference norm is 2.1727e-15 against the threshold 1.3130e-18, relative difference 2.35e-11, in two separate processes. This failure is kept as a failure; the threshold was not widened. Exact resumption of the old 181-dimensional run on this host is therefore not available.
- The unchanged derivative and provider benchmark passes on this host: all 186 raw and 23 feasible finite-difference checks and the 3 adjoint checks meet the unchanged thresholds (largest relative errors 1.01e-10 raw against 3e-5 and 1.34e-9 feasible against 1e-3; adjoint 1.57e-17 against 1e-12; largest trial C5 defect 2.78e-12 against 1e-10). A direct thin SVD of the column-normalized 90,624 x 181 feasible Jacobian keeps all 181 singular values; the smallest is 1.2247e-7 and the condition number is 4.145e7.
- Diagnosis of the replay difference: forcing the other OpenBLAS kernel family gives a bitwise identical Linux residual vector, so that kernel choice does not explain the difference. At 212 selected nodes (origin, middle and outer blocks, including near-axis nodes and the largest gaps), the stored Mac residual and the fresh Linux residual were compared with an 85-digit reference built from the exact binary coefficients. The predeclared diagnostic criterion was that both errors stay within 64 eps S_i, where S_i is the quadrature weight times the absolute sum of the residual's products with every field replaced by the sum of absolute generator contributions. All 212 nodes pass; the largest ratios are 12.53 (Mac), 9.27 (Linux) and 5.35 (Linux minus Mac). Both platforms therefore meet this envelope at these nodes, but the specific arithmetic operation causing the difference was not isolated. This is a diagnostic comparison, not a rigorous forward error bound.
- A precision finding common to both platforms: at the node r = 0.0200273 near the x axis, just outside the Taylor-series radius, R_f = 1.489e-8 carries a relative error of 2.1e-6 on both platforms, from cancellation between generator contributions of size about 273.
- Sampled residual maxima recomputed independently (32 fixed regions, two denser grids and local refinement), with 85-digit radial arithmetic at binary64 angular coordinates and a full 85-digit Cartesian evaluation at the maximizing points: fixed-grid E_f = 1.23849639743e-4 and E_rho = 7.16582921426e-5; refined E_f = 1.23863020848e-4 and E_rho = 7.16586070381e-5, matching the documented values. These are sampled measurements, not continuum maxima.
- Far field of accepted278 recomputed algebraically: ||E_1||_{L2(0,pi/2)} = 0.440353421495, ||E_2|| = 6.36313245370, ||E_3|| = 31.7214716943; the leading unnormalized density residual power is 2 gamma - 1 = 0.105587864228 > 0 (growth). The part of E_1 in odd harmonics above 19 has L2 norm 6.369e-4, which is 0.144639% of ||E_1|| (the historical 0.145222% refers to an earlier state).
- The unchanged independent residual assessment of accepted278 passes on the working copy. A tight whole-axis integral gives J_abs = 4.268372607095585e-9 with an error diagnostic of 2.07e-21, which agrees to 2.2e-22 with the independent 85-digit value. The canonical Poisson inverse check passes (center strain error 7.8e-16), and so do the origin overlap checks. Its sampled maxima agree with the independent recomputation above to a relative 1.6e-11 or better.
- Decision: exact resumption of the 181-dimensional run is not available on this host. Instead, a reviewed new numerical epoch on this host was adopted for the same physical endpoint, as the handoff's Linux deployment notes allow. Its basis is the four qualification results above (rounding diagnostic, objective, benchmark and assessment). All later strict replays of the 860 campaign compare against residual vectors computed on this host. The saved Mac vectors are unchanged, and the strict Mac replay is still reported as FAIL.

## 4. Embedding of the 186 old generators and the feasible dimension
Algebra (verified). The implemented evaluator and the saved 860 seed passed the embedding gate (section 5). The full gate of the implemented feasible chart and of the 855-dimensional spectrum at the seed ran under a frozen code manifest. Its strict verdict is FAIL: 27 of its 29 checks pass, and the two failing checks are the finite-difference checks, which fail only on three directions in the numerically null subspace of the feasible matrix. The campaign proceeds under a recorded exception for those three directions, which give no relative derivative-accuracy evidence (details below). A verified six-point diagnostic of all 855 feasible columns is also reported below.
- Generators. The 860 generators chi_(m,k,j) = r^(2k) (1+r^2)^(p_j-k) cos(2m theta), m = 0..9, k = m..25, j = 0..3, are 860 indexed functions; the parameters at which they become linearly dependent or give zero fields are listed under Exceptional parameters below. They split into a low block (k <= 4, 60 functions) and a high block (800 functions).
- Embedding. All 186 old generators lie exactly in their span, through a coefficient map that does not depend on c_l. The map has rank 186 and was checked with exact rational arithmetic and exact two-sided inverses.
  - Old core functions with sigma = 0, 1, 2 expand binomially into the j = 0 branch.
  - Old core functions with sigma = delta, 2 delta, 3 delta are single generators of the branches j = 1, 2, 3.
  - The core function (m, sigma) = (3, 2) needs three generators.
  - The 162 old Global functions map, in the internal Legendre coordinates of the high block, one to one onto internal coordinates. The internal coordinates use the 26 normalization constants exactly as the source computes them in binary64.
  - An independent high-precision check compared the old representation with its embedding at four values of c_l and 46 points each, including the origin, both axes and log r up to 44. Fields and c_l derivatives agree to 3e-88 and 1.5e-77, where each difference is divided by max(1, |reference value|); these are therefore absolute differences for fields of size below 1, not uniform relative errors of near-zero fields.
- Feasible dimension. For every c_l > 1 and nonzero amplitude a_0, the center relation and the five Taylor conditions have coefficient rank exactly 6. An explicit minor has determinant 480 a_0^2 c_l. Adding c_l as a variable gives (860 + 1) - 6 = 855 feasible variables near any exact feasible point: c_l, 54 further low-block directions and the 800 high-block coefficients. No gauge direction is removed, because the dilation of a state is not in general a vector of the finite span.
- Feasible Jacobian at the seed (diagnostic, verified). Every one of the 855 feasible Jacobian columns of the implemented chart at the seed was compared with an independent 170-digit reference, which used the exact rational constraint derivatives, at six points: r = 0.1, r = 4 and r = e^24 at one angle off the axes, and r = 1 at that angle and on both axes. For each column, the largest error over the six points divided by the column's largest magnitude over the same points is at most 2.9e-14 (comparison level 1e-11), and the whole matrix agrees within 8.3e-17 relative in the Frobenius norm. A second independent recomputation at 150 and 200 digits reproduced the references. This six-point measure dilutes errors at points where a column is small. At r = 0.1 alone, 13 low-block feasible columns have local relative errors between 1.0e-11 and 2.9e-10. The main cause is the error of the production raw Jacobian at that point, amplified by cancellation (by up to a factor of 6.1e4) when the raw low-block columns are combined into the chart. No point lies in the origin-series disk r <= 0.02, the switch between evaluation formulas at r = 1 is sampled from one side only, and only one angle off the axes, one state and one c_l were used. The same six-point comparison at accepted state 10 of the repaired pilot (verified, and reproduced independently for all 60 low raw columns and six high raw columns at the five points off the x axis) found every one of the 860 raw and 855 feasible columns within 1.8e-15 and 1.5e-14 of its scale, and all eight finite-difference directions passed at steps 1e-4, 1e-5 and 1e-6. At r = 0.1, 30 low-block feasible columns of state 10 have local relative errors between 1.1e-11 and 1.84e-9, where their values are small. These are six points at one further state, not a full-mesh or uniform bound. At accepted state 18 the constraint derivative and the chart tangent agree with exact rational reconstructions to relative Frobenius errors of 1.0225e-15 and 2.778e-16; that tangent belongs to the set on which the five Taylor defects keep their saved nonzero values at state 18 (largest 3.81e-14), not to exact feasibility. The same six-point comparison at state 18 found every one of the 860 raw and 855 feasible columns within 2.75e-15 and 1.30e-14 of its scale (comparison level 1e-11); at r = 0.1, 24 of the 55 low-tangent feasible columns have local relative errors between 1.04e-11 and 4.18e-9, where their values are small (absolute errors below 1.454e-13). This is one further state and six points, not a full-mesh bound and not a statement about stationarity. The full chart gate and the spectrum are reported in the next items.
- Feasible Jacobian near the origin (diagnostic, verified). The same comparison was made at 26 points, including two in the origin-series disk r <= 0.02 and literal axis points. At both disk points every one of the 55 low-block feasible columns differs from the high-precision composition by more than 1e-11 relative, up to 1.96e-5 for one column at r = 0.004 in the retained batch composition (1.97e-5 in the stateless composition), with absolute errors near 1e-17. The exact feasible low columns vanish to sixth order at the origin, because the five Taylor conditions remove their degree-2 and degree-4 terms. The binary64 tangent basis satisfies those conditions only to rounding, so each column carries a degree-2 and degree-4 polynomial whose coefficients are at most 1.85e-11. This term accounts for the large relative errors. For the seed and its retained tangent, this polynomial restricted to the disk part of the training mesh has operator norm about 1.15e-17 in the column-scaled feasible matrix (an exact finite-mesh certificate gives less than 1.234e-17). At the default initial trust radius its contribution to the predicted decrease is below 5.3e-26 (exact certificate), against the precision floor of 1.21e-22 (128 eps J at the seed, with eps = 2^-52 and J the training-mesh objective), which the acceptance rule applies to the actual decrease and a stop rule applies to the predicted decrease. This calculation covers neither the full Jacobian error nor later states or larger radii. Just outside the disk, the retained batch feasible columns have band-scaled errors up to 1.1e-9 for radii 0.03 to 0.095; at r = 0.03, the raw-Jacobian part dominates the error in the examined column 10. Jacobian errors can change the proposed step and the predicted decrease, and through the predicted decrease the gain ratio, the integration-uncertainty test, the acceptance decision, the radius update, the mesh-refinement stop and the two stops taken before a trial is evaluated: the precision-floor stop (predicted decrease at or below 128 eps J) and the rounding-bound stop (predicted decrease below about 40 times the bound on the rounding error of the incumbent's computed objective). Their small size does not prove that any of these decisions is unchanged. They do not enter the actual decrease of a trial, which is computed from the residual evaluated at the lifted trial point, and a trial is accepted only if that actual decrease exceeds the precision floor and the rounding bound.
- Full feasible-chart gate at the seed (strict verdict FAIL, with a recorded exception). The gate ran under a frozen code manifest for 13,050 s of wall-clock time on the assigned cores, with a peak memory of 120,629 MiB. 27 of its 29 checks pass. Most of them are qualification checks of provenance, shapes, numerical ranks and replay, not of derivative accuracy. They include the numerical ranks of the center relation and the five Taylor conditions (5 of 5 for the reduced conditions, 6 of 6 with the center relation), the ranks 55 and 60 of the low-block tangent and of its completion, the containment of the old 19-dimensional low tangent in the new 55-dimensional tangent and in the 855-dimensional feasible space (within 1.2e-15), three lifts back to the constraint set (Taylor-condition defects at most 1.8e-13 against 1e-10), tangency of the dilation rows (3.9e-19 against 1e-10), bitwise reproduction of the seed residual by a zero step through the chart and through the campaign trial path (with the residual recomputed, not read from a cache), and the matrix shape 90,624 x 855 with all 855 computed singular values positive.
  - Finite differences. The frozen test compares central differences of the residual at steps 1e-4, 1e-5 and 1e-6 with the matrix action A v along 26 directions. Its measure is the relative error ||FD - A v|| / ||A v||, against 1e-3 for 10 directions through the chart and 3e-5 for 16 single high-block coordinates; each lifted point must also meet the Taylor conditions within 1e-10. 23 directions pass at every step. Their largest relative errors are 1.7e-8 for the chart directions (c_l along the chart, five low-block tangent vectors and the projected dilation) and 1.4e-12 for the high-block coordinates. Lifted Taylor defects are at most 3.7e-13 for all 26 directions.
  - The two failing checks are this test at the best step and at all steps. Both fail only on the three right singular vectors of the three smallest computed singular values. For these directions ||A v|| is 2.5e-15 to 3.6e-15, at the rounding level eps sigma_max = 2.1e-15 of the matrix, and the relative errors are 2.2e3 to 6.8e4. Their absolute discrepancies ||FD - A v|| are 5.5e-12 to 1.2e-11 at the best step and at most 2.5e-10 at any step. These are smaller than those of the passing c_l and low-block tangent directions, which are 1.1e-10 to 2.8e-10 at the best step and up to 2.4e-8 at any step. The discrepancy grows by a factor of 15 to 26 as the step falls from 1e-4 to 1e-6, consistent with finite-precision effects amplified by division by the step. The saved record does not separate their sources. The product of the step and the discrepancy is 8.5e-17 to 1.2e-15, the level that the passing c_l and low-block tangent directions show at the two smaller steps (3.9e-16 to 2.8e-15). A pass at 1e-3 would need a discrepancy below 2.5e-18 to 3.6e-18. The observed discrepancies exceed that by more than six orders of magnitude, and the smallest discrepancy of any chart direction at any step is 1.6e-12. These observations do not prove that no evaluation of these directions could pass the frozen test.
  - A separate diagnostic of the weakest directions (diagnostic, computed and read back by the verification session; its algebraic basis was reviewed independently). The verification session took the three weakest right singular vectors of its own assembly of the same matrix and formed central differences at steps 1e-4, 1e-5 and 1e-6 through the frozen chart. It evaluated them at six unweighted points with 170-digit arithmetic. All nine endpoint pairs keep c_l exactly fixed and have their exact midpoint at the seed, and the lift made no normal correction. At fixed c_l the residual is quadratic in the coefficient coordinates, so the exact central difference equals the matrix action along the exact secant of the rounded endpoints, not along the intended direction. In all nine cases the contribution from rounding the coordinates when the step is added to the seed exceeds the sum of all other contributions, residual evaluation included, by a factor of 190 to 1800. The gate saved neither its vectors nor its endpoints. So this six-point calculation does not establish the source of the gate's discrepancies over the full training mesh. It shows a mechanism that dominates in a closely related calculation.
  - Ruling. The failure is kept as a failure. No threshold was changed, no rerun is authorized, and no new test was chosen after seeing these data, because such a test would be post hoc. The frozen test was stricter than the specification, which asked only that the convergence ratio and best agreement be reported for the weakest singular directions; the coordinating session approved the frozen test without noticing this. Under the recorded exception, the three directions supply the absolute discrepancies above but no relative derivative-accuracy evidence, and every other result of the gate stands. The derivative evidence is the 23 passing directions and the earlier per-generator high-precision checks (section 5); the other passing checks qualify provenance, shapes, numerical ranks and replay. The exception does not let the matrix decide acceptance: a trial is accepted on the residual evaluated at the lifted trial point and on independent validation, not on the matrix. This is not an unconditional guarantee against a false acceptance, and no accuracy certificate for the campaign follows from these figures. At the default initial radius the trust step multiplies the residual component of each mode below the diagnostic floor 1.94e-10 by at most s/mu < 1.5e-9 (mu = 0.129). At larger radii mu is smaller and these modes can carry more of the step (next item but one).
- Spectrum of the feasible matrix at the seed (diagnostic). The column-normalized 90,624 x 855 feasible matrix has largest singular value 9.62. The computed singular values fall to 0.245 at index 100, 2.1e-3 at index 200, 3.0e-7 at index 300 and 6.0e-14 at index 400. 504 of the 855 lie below the diagnostic floor max(M, N) eps sigma_max = 1.94e-10, 429 lie below eps sigma_max, the median is 2.1e-15 and the smallest is 4.1e-18. The 855 values are bitwise equal to those of a separate thin SVD of the same matrix, assembled again with the frozen evaluator by the verification session (normalized matrix SHA-256 bee5163b...). A second route (the compact route: QR reduction followed by an SVD of the triangular factor) agrees within 1.3e-14 in absolute value. Positivity of computed binary64 singular values is not a rank certificate and gives no lower bound on the singular values of the matrix; the count of values above the floor (351) is a diagnostic, not a rank. The analytic derivative at the seed is injective (exact result below), while 429 singular values of the sampled binary64 matrix lie below eps sigma_max, where ordinary normwise binary64 error estimates do not certify that they are positive. The injectivity of the analytic derivative gives no lower bound for this sampled matrix.
- Exact finite trust-step checks at the seed (verified, exact; the proofs were reviewed and every number was recomputed independently). For the saved binary64 matrix and residual, trust steps were computed by the campaign trust-step routine from the direct SVD and from the compact route. At the default initial radius 4.62e-5 both satisfy the production KKT definition: exact rational calculations of eta squared give relative residuals eta of about 1.28e-14 and 1.67e-14. Their exact quadratic-model trust gap is below 1.39e-22, which is 3.4e-13 of the predicted reduction 4.10e-10; almost all of it comes from the radius slack of the stored step. At radius 1000 the two steps have eta of about 2.5e-7 and 2.8e-7, above the linear trust-step KKT threshold 1e-8, and the available certificate bounds their trust gap only by the model value at the step, 7.35e-14, which is 1.7e-5 of the predicted reduction. They differ by 0.0396 in norm (relative 4e-5), and the full-matrix response of that difference has norm 2.2e-12. About 76 percent of the squared step difference, resolved in the direct right-singular-vector basis, lies in modes with singular values below 1e-4 sqrt(mu), where sqrt(mu) = 6.0e-11 is the damping. The 496 modes at or below the damping carry 46.5 percent of the squared step norm at that radius in both routes; this is a statement about one finite matrix and one radius, and it is less than half. These facts concern a fixed binary64 matrix and its quadratic model. They are not nonlinear stationarity or a PDE solution, and no cause is attributed to the difference between the two routes.
- Exceptional parameters. At fixed c_l the field map from the 860 coefficients is injective except at c_l = 2 (a 3-dimensional kernel of quadratic potentials) and c_l = 3 (208 zero-field directions, 205 of them from exact coincidences between branches 0 and 3). These are statements about finite linear spaces. They are not ranks of the residual Jacobian.
- Exact injectivity of the analytic residual derivative at the seed (verified, exact). Read c_l, the 59 free low-block coefficients, the 800 high-block coefficients and the 26 normalization constants of the saved seed as exact rational numbers (their binary64 values), and fix the remaining low-block coefficient by the center relation exactly. At this rational point, the derivative of the analytic residual R_f with respect to c_l and the 860 coefficients, all 861 treated as independent variables, is injective. So is its restriction to the 855-dimensional kernel of the derivatives of the center relation and the five Taylor conditions. Proof: in compressed homogeneous coordinates q = x^2+y^2 and z = x^2/q, the Taylor coefficients of the linearized R_f through degree 112 form a 3120 by 861 rational matrix; the omitted angular rows vanish. Modulo the prime 65537 it has an 861 by 861 minor with determinant 49046. A nonzero determinant modulo p implies a nonzero rational determinant (at degree 96 the rank modulo p was 851, which proves nothing). The matrix was built with exact number-theoretic-transform convolutions and read back independently. Every entry was reproduced by a second construction (Cartesian differentiation along 21 rays). A third, separate computation recomputed all 861 columns from the defining formulas in Cartesian monomials through degree 40 in x^2 and y^2 and found 0 mismatches in 741321 entries; a separate elimination of the minor gave the same determinant. Scope: this is a statement about the derivative of the exact analytic residual at one rational point, and the Taylor defects of that point are small but not zero. It says nothing about the sampled training-mesh matrix, gives no lower bound on singular values or conditioning, and is neither a stationarity result nor an exact PDE solution.
- Exactly Taylor-feasible rational reference next to the seed (verified, exact; not a campaign checkpoint). The five Taylor conditions with the center relation can be solved in closed form: for any c_l > 1, amplitude a != 0 and two further free density jets, the other jets follow by rational formulas, so the conditions fix neither c_l nor a. Starting from the exact rational seed of the previous item, a change of nine low-block coefficients (branch 0, k = 3 and 4), with c_l, a, the two free jets and every other coefficient held fixed, makes the center relation and all five Taylor conditions hold exactly. The change is below 4e-17 in every raw coefficient and below 2.2e-14 in every field jet. Some of the new coefficients are not binary64 numbers, so the corrected potential is a mathematical reference that the campaign code cannot store. At this point the corresponding 3120 by 861 matrix was computed anew (the seed's determinant is not carried over); an 861 by 861 minor has determinant 10361 modulo 65537. So the derivative of R_f is again injective in all 861 independent variables and on the 855-dimensional exactly feasible tangent. The matrix was read back by a separate elimination and, in all 2,043,153 coefficients, by Cartesian differentiation along 21 formal rays. The whole-axis objective J_abs changes by an exactly computed negative amount of about -2.08e-22 (relative size 5e-14). For the far field of this reference see section 8. Injectivity therefore does not depend on the small nonzero Taylor defects of the seed. Scope: as for the previous item; this says nothing about optimizer states, the sampled training-mesh matrix or an exact PDE solution.
- Exact feasible descent from the rational reference (verified, exact; not a campaign checkpoint). Starting from the exactly Taylor-feasible reference of the previous item (J_abs = 4.268372607095e-9), explicit rational changes of branch-0 coefficients with k = 3 and 4 give a two-parameter surface chi_0 + s A + s^2 B + t V. On it the center relation and all five Taylor conditions hold exactly for every real s and t, with c_l and a fixed. Along the line s = 0, the change of J_abs from the reference is an exact quartic in t with rational coefficients. With t fixed at the rational midpoint of the enclosure of alpha (below), the change from the state at s = 0 is an exact octic in s, again with rational coefficients. Every perturbation term carries a radial factor r^2/(1+r^2), so each integral in these changes is a convergent beta integral with a rational value. The value at the reference itself comes from the earlier whole-axis objective computation, which encloses it but does not show it to be rational.
  - On the line s = 0, J_abs has a unique global minimum at t = alpha, about 1.0868e-4, with J_abs about 4.265604690193e-9. The exact rational state t = 1/16384 on this line has J_abs about 4.266136640745e-9.
  - The derivative of J_abs in s at t = alpha is strictly negative (about -5.946e-9), so the minimum on the line is not stationary in the 860 family.
  - With t fixed at a rational point inside the enclosure of alpha, the exact rational state s = 2^-19 has J_abs about 4.265597848970e-9. This is below the minimum on the whole line. The unique minimum along this second curve, at s about 2.4035e-6, has J_abs about 4.265597544432e-9.
  - At that second-curve minimum the derivative of J_abs in t is strictly positive (about 2.2411e-9; an exact degree-six polynomial in s with rational coefficients, enclosed over the certified bracket for the minimum). Decreasing t from that state therefore strictly decreases J_abs on the same exactly feasible surface, so this minimum is not stationary in the 860 family either.
  - The total decrease from the reference is about 2.78e-12, or 6.5e-4 relative.
  - Independent whole-axis quadratures corroborate the displayed objective values and the earlier quartic, transverse cubic and octic coefficients to about 1e-28 relative in the saved 30-digit displays. For the return-direction polynomial, g1 through g6 agree to better than 4e-33 relative internally; its near-zero constant g0 agrees only to about 1.3e-38 absolutely. Separately, independent exact rational replays reproduce every sign, discriminant and bound.
  - Scope: these are exact Taylor-feasible states, or certified algebraic minima on one line or one curve. None of the five states is stationary in the family: the reference and the two rational states lie strictly before the unique minimum of an exactly feasible line or curve through them, where the derivative of J_abs is strictly negative, and each of the two algebraic minima has a certified strictly decreasing feasible direction. None is a campaign checkpoint or an optimizer-accepted step, and some of the new coefficients are not binary64 numbers. None is an exact PDE solution. In the two rational states t = 1/16384 and s = 2^-19 the far field still grows (section 8); there is no far-field computation for the two algebraic minima.
- Certified whole-plane bound for one exact state (verified, exact; not a campaign checkpoint). For the exact rational second-curve state s = 2^-19 of the previous item (t at the rational midpoint), R_f is exactly a finite sum of terms q^(ell delta) P(1 - q) cos(n theta), with q = 1/(1+r^2), delta = (1 - 1/c_l)/2, ell = 1 to 8, even n up to 38, and polynomials P with 2089 rational coefficients in total. Rounding these coefficients to multiples of 2^-192, covering 2^-40 <= q <= 1 by 167 rational intervals with rational enclosures of the powers q^(ell delta), and bounding the remaining range q < 2^-40 exactly gives a rational number U_R with |R_f| <= U_R at every r >= 0 and every angle. Every step uses rational arithmetic only. U_R/a_ref is at most 2.0184e-4. So the suprema over the whole plane of |R_f|/a_ref and |R_rho|/(r a_ref) for this state are at most 2.0184e-4. A single value is a lower bound for the supremum, and two exact values give separate lower bounds for the two quotients. On the horizontal symmetry axis (theta = 0) at r = e^2.16 (about 8.67), an exact rational enclosure of R_f, computed by two independent routes, gives |R_f|/a_ref approximately 1.243019e-4, certified to exceed 1.2430e-4. So the supremum of |R_f|/a_ref lies strictly between 1.2430e-4 and 2.0184e-4. On that axis sin(theta) = 0 and the second quotient vanishes, so this point gives no lower bound for |R_rho|/(r a_ref). On the vertical axis (theta = pi/2) at r = e^5.6 (about 270), where sin(theta) = 1 and the two quotients are equal, the same kind of exact enclosure gives R_f/a_ref approximately 7.16517e-5, certified to exceed 7.159e-5. So the supremum of |R_rho|/(r a_ref) lies strictly between 7.159e-5 and 2.0184e-4. Neither point is claimed to be where its quotient is largest. A second certificate for the same state also subdivides the angle: 845 closed rational cells in q and x = cos(2 theta), each bounded through an exact polynomial in both variables, with the density quotient bounded through sin^2(theta) = (1 - x)/2. It narrows the two brackets to 1.2430e-4 < sup |R_f|/a_ref < 1.2992e-4 and 7.159e-5 < sup |R_rho|/(r a_ref) < 7.4842e-5. Every cell bound was recomputed independently by two further algebraic routes and gave the same rational numbers. A search on a fine grid in log r and angle found no larger value of |R_f|. Every interval bound, the remaining-range bound and the cover were recomputed independently by a different algebraic route and gave the same rational numbers. Scope: one exact rational state. This gives no bound on the unnormalized R_rho, which grows at infinity for this state (section 8). It says nothing about optimizer states and is not a distance to an exact PDE solution.

## 5. Reproduction of the baseline by the 860 seed

Status: passed. Every step of the embedding gate passed under the strict verdict below and was independently checked, within the scopes stated below. One earlier supplement step failed on a single check whose premise predated the repairs. That step is recorded as superseded, and the replacement step passed.

Figures are rounded to two significant digits. A figure written after "within", "at most" or "worst" is rounded upward, so it is a true bound on the recorded value; other figures are approximate.

How the gate was run. Every gate step ran under a frozen code manifest. The manifest holds the SHA-256 of every library file, test script and runner. Before each step the runner recomputed all hashes and refused to start if any differed. During one step, the rounding calibration, two utility scripts listed in its manifest (a manifest-freezing script and a summary script) were edited. Neither was executed by that step, and the library, test and loaded-module hashes were unchanged throughout. Both edits are recorded with their before and after hashes. The earlier bytes of the manifest-freezing script were reconstructed exactly, but the earlier bytes of the summary script were not kept. During the replacement supplement step, two more listed files were edited: the runner and the self-test driver of the next gate. Neither was executed by that step, and its runner, test and loaded-module hashes were unchanged. Their earlier bytes were recovered exactly and match the hashes in the manifest. For these two steps, therefore, the claim is that every executed file was unchanged, not that every listed file was. Steps ran under a resource guard on the assigned cores at low priority. Each step wrote a JSON record of individual checks. A step passed only if all three held: the runner verdict was ok, the record's passed field was true, and no individual check failed. Every record was read independently by the coordinating session and by the verification session. Supplements that added test files left all earlier library hashes unchanged, which was checked file by file.

Defects found and repaired before the gate passed. None of these was tolerated by widening a tolerance.
- The origin Taylor series at order 20 truncated high-block generators by up to about 4.2e-3 of their own scale for r <= 0.02. An exact rational truncation bound shows order 40 is enough. Production uses order 48, which is checked against order 56.
- For the j = 0 branch at large r, the direct linear transport term cancelled its leading power, losing about 2 log10 r digits. The fix is the exact identity L F(chi_mkj) = -j (c_l - 1) F(chi_mkj) + 2 c_l (k - p_j) F(chi_mkj / (1 + r^2)), which has no such cancellation.
- The original radial derivative route lost accuracy in two places:
  - seven high-block values outside the origin disk, by about 1.1e-11 to 1.9e-10 of their band scale;
  - generators with the integer exponent p_j = 1 at c_l = 2 and 3 and at nearby values, by up to about 9.2e-5 of the band scale.
  Radial derivatives are now formed in falling-factorial form, with the exponent differences evaluated from their rational identity in extended precision, so factors that are exactly zero stay exactly zero.
- In 0.02 <= r < 10 the seed residual R_f is a near-cancellation between linear and nonlinear transport. Its binary64 rounding error therefore scales with those terms, not with R_f, and depended on the batch shape of the evaluation. A relaxed tolerance was proposed and then withdrawn. R_f is now assembled in extended precision (64-bit significand), using a center-subtracted strain ratio for r < 1 and the stable linear form at large r. It is gated at 1e-11 of the band maximum in every band.

Band rule. A quantity's error is divided by max(band_max, 1e-30). Here band_max is the largest reference magnitude of that quantity over the sample points of its radial band. The bands are r < 0.02, [0.02, 0.1), [0.1, 1), [1, 10), [10, 1e3), [1e3, e^16) and [e^16, 2.6e10). Where band_max < 1e-30 the tolerance is therefore an absolute 1e-41. Every such case is listed in the records.

Verified results of the frozen library:
- Coefficient map and seed constants (58 checks):
  - Generator identities at 85 digits agree within 3.4e-52 of their scale.
  - The low-block production evaluator agrees with a 90-digit reference within 6.0e-15 of the summed term size for values and 2.2e-14 for c_l derivatives.
  - The eliminated center coefficient a_000 equals b_00 + b_01 + b_02 within 8.3e-89.
  - The seed's a_0 agrees with the baseline within 3.8e-17 relative. W_x and W_y agree within 1.6e-15 and 3.1e-15.
  - The seed's five raw Taylor defects are at most 8.0e-14 in absolute value (limit 1e-12; the baseline's own maximum is about 4.9e-14).
- Fields and c_l derivatives of embedded old coefficient profiles against the 181-dimensional evaluator: two profiles at each of c_l = 1.80899, 1.5, 2.5 and 4.0, at 84 points per profile (27 checks). This test compares assembled profiles, not each old generator separately.
  - worst field difference 7.6e-13 of the band scale (limit 1e-11);
  - worst c_l derivative difference 7.7e-14;
  - high-precision spot values at r = e^24, e^32 and e^44 within 1.4e-16.
- Every raw Jacobian column (c_l, 59 free low, 800 high) and R_f against high-precision references, at point triples in each band, two origin points and r = e^24, e^32, e^44 and the outer mesh radius (152 checks):
  - R_f worst 7.1e-14 of the band maximum and columns worst 6.6e-13, both in [0.02, 0.1);
  - origin columns 4.1e-14;
  - outer radii: R_f at most 1.2e-16 and columns at most 2.6e-15.
  Every evaluation configuration (one point, one batch, production chunking with shifted chunk boundaries, and the integrator's entry point) gave R_f bitwise equal to the one-point call. The training-mesh residual under shifted chunking agrees within 0.0032 of the 64 eps replay tolerance. At c_l = 2, 3, 2(1 + 2^-20) and 3(1 - 2^-20), R_f and all columns are within 2.6e-13 in every band.
- All 860 generators, the seven fields (f, f_x, f_y, psi, psi_x, psi_y, psi_x/y) and their c_l derivatives, compared with 85-digit references at c_l = 1.02, 2, 3, 10, 2(1 +- 2^-20) and 3(1 +- 2^-20) (20 checks). There are nine sample points: one at a generic angle in each of the seven bands, plus r = 0.0199 and r = 0.0201 in the first two bands. Worst 3.9e-13 for fields and 4.4e-13 for derivatives. All floor cases are listed: 416, 562 and 1323 cases at c_l = 2, 3 and 10.
- Twelve values of c_l next to 2 and 3 (both binary64 neighbours, and offsets of relative size 2^-30 and 2^-40 on each side), with the radii where the near-integer exponent changes the balance of terms added as sample points (24 checks in each of two runs): worst 3.8e-13 for fields and 5.0e-13 for c_l derivatives over all points. In the second run each added point was also gated on its own, per kind of radius and per field, for values and c_l derivatives (504 entries); every entry was at most 2.3e-16.

Reproduction of the baseline by the seed (T3), verified. The 860 seed is the baseline accepted278 written in the 860 basis. Its residual was evaluated with the repaired 860 evaluator and compared with a fresh evaluation by the qualified 181-dimensional evaluator on the same nodes:
- Training mesh (90,624 nodes): relative residual difference about 3.3e-11 (limit 1e-9); largest pointwise |Delta R_f| / a_ref about 7.5e-15 (limit 1e-12).
- Refined mesh: about 3.2e-11 and 8.5e-15.
- The block with r > e^16 alone: relative difference about 2.7e-10 (limit 1e-9).
- The Jacobian chain rule holds in every one of the 186 old columns: the 860 Jacobian composed with the embedding reproduces each column within 1.4e-14 of its norm, and no column needed the absolute floor. In the r > e^16 block alone, every column met the rule with the floor of 1e-13 times the largest column norm there (about 3.8e-16), but at least one column of small norm in that block passed only by the floor (its relative difference reached 1.07e9), and the T3 record did not list which. A later independent check recomputed the whole block (all 24,576 nodes and 186 columns, in six blocks on one thread, so it is not a bitwise replay of the T3 step). Exactly four columns pass only by the floor, with relative differences of about 1.1e9, 6.2e8, 2.3e-5 and 1.3e-5. At their worst nodes these columns are nearly zero. For the three worst, compared with an independent 170-digit reference at those nodes, the 181-dimensional values agree within 4.6e-15 relative. The 860 values, which are raw columns composed through the embedding, have absolute errors of at most 8.5e-19, at most 3.3e-16 of the summed magnitude of the composed raw terms. So the 860 values are accurate in absolute terms and lose relative accuracy only through this cancellation, and no relative agreement is claimed for these four columns. The fourth column was not compared with an independent reference. This does not contradict the 860 error levels qualified above, so the gate stands. The supplement step below separately confirms selected far-radius comparisons of R_f and of Jacobian columns.
- Independent whole-axis objective J_abs: 4.268372607095638e-9 for the 860 seed and 4.268372607095585e-9 for the baseline. The difference is about 5.3e-23. The criterion is |J_860 - J_181| <= 1e-9 J plus the sum of the two integrators' error estimates, here about 4.27e-18. The error sum, about 2.7e-21, is itself below 1e-9 J, so the comparison is conclusive. The error estimates are diagnostics of the integrators, not certified bounds.
- Sampled maxima over the frozen 32 assessment regions: E_f = 1.2384963974265792e-4 and E_rho = 7.165829214261253e-5, both within 3.1e-12 relative of the baseline (limit 1e-10). In one region, r <= 0.08, the regional maxima differ by about 1.4e-9 relative. At every point diagnosed there (five named points at four distinct locations), the 860 value is within 1.8e-18 of an 85-digit reference, while the 181-dimensional value is off by 3.0e-14 to 7.1e-14. An independent 170-digit evaluation confirmed this. The difference is therefore the older evaluator's error near the origin, and the region is excused under the stated rule.
- The saved seed reloads in a fresh process and reproduces its residual bitwise (the strict criterion is 64 eps). A repeat evaluation in the same process is also bitwise equal, and so is the stateless provider used by the independent integrator.

Supplement step (12 checks):
- Fresh processes with (worker count, cache-construction thread count) = (1, 1), (4, 4), (8, 4), (8, 8) and (8, 4) again reproduce the training-mesh residual of the saved seed byte for byte. The SHA-256 of the residual bytes is identical in all five runs.
- The coefficient arrays of the replay reference equal those of the sealed seed byte for byte. The three seed files are read only and match their recorded hashes.
- All 860 generators, their fields and their c_l derivatives at c_l = 1.809 and 2.5 agree with 85-digit references within 3.2e-14 of the band scale (limit 1e-11).
- The repaired R_f at r = e^32 and e^44 agrees with a 50-digit reference within 6.0e-17 of the band maximum (limit 1e-11). Spot Jacobian columns at e^24 agree within 7.5e-16. The older 181-dimensional route is off by about 1.3e-5 of the band maximum at e^44. This is reported as a diagnostic only, and that route is not used in production.
- The independent integral gives J_abs = 4.268372607095638e-9, the T3 value, with error estimate 6.1e-22. The frozen-mesh rule under radial dilations of 0.999, 1.001 and 2 differs from it by at most 1.9e-14 relative (limit 1e-9). The part of J from 24 < log r <= 68 is about 4.4e-15 of J. For the exact analytic seed, an exact rational bound shows that the part beyond log r = 68 is below 6.7e-45, that is below 1.6e-36 of J. This bound covers neither the floating-point evaluator nor the quadrature error.

Origin series at two fixed states (verified). For r <= 0.02 the evaluator computes R_f from its Taylor polynomial. Two fixed coefficient states at the seed value of c_l were examined: the seed, and a test state that keeps the seed's low block and sets the i-th of the 800 high coefficients to (-1)^i (1 + (i mod 7)) 2^-30. The test state is not an optimizer state.
- Truncation. Exact rational arithmetic bounds the difference between R_f and its exact order-48 Taylor polynomial on the disk r <= 0.02 by 1.1e-60 (seed) and 1.5e-61 (test state). The polynomial was constructed independently, by ordinary Cartesian differentiation of the full nonlinear residual, and agrees exactly with the coefficients the bound uses.
- Evaluation. Evaluating the series in extended precision and rounding the result to binary64 costs far more than the truncation. Assume the standard model of basic floating-point operations: each multiplication and addition has relative error at most one unit of the 64-bit significand, with no underflow or overflow (the latter was checked for the sampled inputs). Under that assumption the total error at 121 sampled nodes per state, including the final rounding, is at most 4.7e-24 (seed) and 4.4e-25 (test state). The model of the host arithmetic is assumed, not proved, and the final rounding is bounded only at the sampled nodes.
- Uniform evaluation bound (verified, conditional). Under explicit models of the extended-precision additions and multiplications (relative error at most 2^-63 plus an absolute error at most 2^-16445, which allows gradual underflow, with a 64-bit working significand), of the final round-to-nearest conversion to binary64 (relative error at most 2^-53 plus an absolute error at most 2^-1075), and of the radius test (computed radius within 2^-52 r + 2^-1074 of the exact radius r), and provided the call uses the modeled operation sequence and reproduces the retained converted coefficients, the binary64 value returned by the origin-series evaluator differs from the exact R_f by less than 1.67e-23 (seed) and 1.37e-24 (test state) at every binary64 point that the radius test admits, including the origin, the axes and subnormal coordinates. The radius test admits some binary64 points whose exact radius slightly exceeds 0.02, so the bound is proved on the disk enlarged by the relative factor 1 + 2^-50, which contains every admitted point under the radius-test model. If the final conversion is only known to be within 2^-52 relative plus 2^-1074, the bounds are 3e-23 and 2.5e-24. These models are assumptions about the hardware and the libraries, not proven properties. That the production code performs the modeled operation sequence was established by reading the code, not by a formal semantics. All 648 converted coefficients of the two states were reproduced, but a different decimal rounding context or a recomputed coefficient table would need that check again. The bound covers point values of R_f only, not the weighted residual, the derivative columns or the integrals.
- Integral. The whole-axis integrator takes the contribution of the disk r <= 0.02 to J_abs from the order-56 polynomial. At these two states, unit dilation and the radius equal to the binary64 value of 0.02, the value it returns differs from the exact contribution of R_f by at most 1.4e-35 (seed) and 1.7e-37 (test state). This bound needs no model of the floating-point arithmetic, because the returned binary64 value is compared exactly with the exact integral of the order-56 polynomial. It applies to this summation order only. The difference between the order-48 and order-56 values that the integrator records for this piece is exactly zero, and it is not an error bound.
None of these bounds covers other states, such as those the optimizer will visit, the c_l derivative columns, or the rest of the integral.

These are checks of the evaluator and of the embedding at fixed parameter states. They are not a bound on the residual of any later optimizer state. No bound of this kind exists for any optimizer state. At the optimizer states the evaluator was checked by independent recomputation instead: the J_abs of every accepted state of the repaired pilot was reproduced by an independent quadrature (section 7), and the derivative columns were compared at six points at the seed and at accepted states 10 and 18 (section 4).

## 6. Verified research and compute hours
Status: figures at the data cut 2026-10-26T04:00:00Z, reproduced independently from the run records (5,129 checks, and a separate check of the 684 successful guarded runs). Work after the cut is counted only in the figures of the second, time-only cut 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z, given after the earlier cutoffs below; that cut adds no state, trial or result and only measures time.

The isolated campaign began at 2026-10-24T03:05Z when the working copy was created, so the 48-hour mark is 2026-10-26T03:05Z. Three working sessions ran in parallel: computation, independent verification and coordination. Wall-clock time, session time, recorded compute time and optimizer time are different quantities and are reported separately. None is the sum of another.

End of the run. The working sessions and all campaign processes were stopped between 2026-10-26T13:28:17Z and 13:29:02Z, at the run's time cap. T_end = 2026-10-26T13:43:03Z, so the elapsed wall-clock time from T0 is 58.63 h. The figures below stop at the time-only cut 2026-10-26T08:00:00Z: the computation after it (the long run until its termination, and the closing checks) is not counted and was not reproduced independently. Whether the prompt's 48-hour genuine campaign was met: both the elapsed time and the recorded computation union exceeded 48 h, but no recorded quantity measures useful work or active investigator time, so 48 hours of genuine work is not established quantitatively. That the campaign met the commitment is a judgment from the dated worklogs, notes, reviews and closed checks of the three working sessions, stated as a judgment and not as a measurement.

| Quantity | Value at 04:00:00Z | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| Elapsed wall-clock time since campaign start | 48.92 h | UTC timestamps |
| Time with at least one working session open | 48.92 h | session supervisor log; a session lifetime is not proof of continuous activity |
| Recorded compute, wall time with at least one recorded run active | 45.04 h | interval union over all runs, see below |
| Recorded compute, sum of the wall times of all recorded runs | 62.75 h | parallel runs counted separately |
| Optimizer worker wall time of completed slices | 20.20 h | the two slices of the closed first pilot (including initialization and blocked intervals), slices 1, 2, 3, 5 and 6 of the repaired pilot and slices 7 to 21, the first fifteen slices of the long run (including initialization and recovery); the fourth slice, ended by the planned SIGKILL test, is not a completed slice |
